# META
~~~ini
description=Self and inverse featuring references use canonical occurrence contexts across reference-value chains, including Base::things::that from KerML 9.2.2
type=file
libraries=standard
require_complete_publication=true
require_no_diagnostics=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package FeaturingContext {
    part def Child;
    part def Leaf;
    requirement def Required { subject root : Root; }
    requirement required : Required;
    part def Root {
        satisfy required by that;
        part child : Child {
            ref part parent : Root = that;
            ref part current : Child = self;
            part leaf : Leaf {
                ref part root : Root = that.that;
                ref part parent : Child = that;
            }
        }
        ref part owner : Root = child.that;
    }
}
~~~
